<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Translingual">Translingual</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: Translingual"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/English_language" class="extiw" title="w:English language">English</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/Dia#English" title="Dia">Dia</a></i>.

<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="mul">dia</strong></span>
</p>
<ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_standard" class="extiw" title="w:International standard">international standards</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> <i><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_639-3" class="extiw" title="w:ISO 639-3">ISO 639-3</a> <a href="/wiki/language_code" title="language code">language code</a> for </i><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_639:dia" class="extiw" title="w:ISO 639:dia"><b>Dia</b></a><i>.</i></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Betawi">Betawi</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=14" title="Edit section: Betawi"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Alternative_forms_2">Alternative forms</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=15" title="Edit section: Alternative forms"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><span class="Latn" lang="bew"><a href="/w/index.php?title=diah&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="diah (page does not exist)">diah</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="bew"><a href="/w/index.php?title=jiah&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="jiah (page does not exist)">jiah</a></span> <span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content">Bekasi</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></li>
<li><span class="Latn" lang="bew"><a href="/wiki/die#Betawi" title="die">die</a></span> <span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content">Tanah Abang</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></li>
<li><span class="Latn" lang="bew"><a href="/wiki/di%C3%A8#Betawi" title="diè">diè</a></span> <span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content">Meester</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_4">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=16" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Malay_language" class="extiw" title="w:Malay language">Malay</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ms"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Malay">dia</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Malayic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Malayic language">Proto-Malayic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="poz-mly-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Malayic/ia" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Malayic/ia">&#42;ia</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Malayo-Polynesian_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Malayo-Polynesian language">Proto-Malayo-Polynesian</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="poz-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Malayo-Polynesian/(si-)ia" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Malayo-Polynesian/(si-)ia">&#42;(si-)ia</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Austronesian_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Austronesian language">Proto-Austronesian</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="map-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Austronesian/(si-)ia" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Austronesian/(si-)ia">&#42;(si-)ia</a></i>.
</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronoun_3">Pronoun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=18" title="Edit section: Pronoun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="bew">dia</strong></span>
</p>
<ol><li><a href="/wiki/he" title="he">he</a>; <a href="/wiki/she" title="she">she</a> (<span class="use-with-mention">third-person singular pronoun</span>)
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="bew"><b>Dia</b> lagi ngelempus.</i><dl><dd><span class="e-translation">He is sleeping now.</span></dd></dl></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="bew"><b>Dia</b> demen ama tu orang.</i><dl><dd><span class="e-translation">She loves him/that person.</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></li></ol>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Synonyms">Synonyms</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=19" title="Edit section: Synonyms"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><span class="Latn" lang="bew"><a href="/w/index.php?title=ni_orang&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="ni orang (page does not exist)">ni orang</a></span></li>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Catalan">Catalan</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=20" title="Edit section: Catalan"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Alternative_forms_3">Alternative forms</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=21" title="Edit section: Alternative forms"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><span class="Latn" lang="ca"><a href="/wiki/d%C3%ADa#Catalan" title="día">día</a></span> <span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#obsolete" title="Appendix:Glossary">obsolete</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_5">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=22" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#inherited" title="Appendix:Glossary">Inherited</a> from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vulgar_Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Vulgar Latin">Vulgar Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Latin/dia" title="Reconstruction:Latin/dia">&#42;dia</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Latin">Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/dies#Latin" title="dies">diēs</a></i>, ultimately from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Indo-European_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Indo-European language">Proto-Indo-European</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ine-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/dy%E1%B8%97ws" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/dyḗws">&#42;dyḗws</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">heaven, sky</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>. Compare <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gascon_dialect" class="extiw" title="w:Gascon dialect">Gascon</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="oc"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Occitan">dia</a></i> and <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spanish_language" class="extiw" title="w:Spanish language">Spanish</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="es"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Spanish">dia</a></i>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation_3">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=23" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Catalan_pronunciation" title="Appendix:Catalan pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content qualifier-content"><span class="usage-label-accent"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Central_Catalan" class="extiw" title="w:Central Catalan">Central</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Balearic_Catalan" class="extiw" title="w:Balearic Catalan">Balearic</a></span></span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span> <span class="IPA nowrap">[ˈdi.ə]</span></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Catalan_pronunciation" title="Appendix:Catalan pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content qualifier-content"><span class="usage-label-accent"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Valencian_language" class="extiw" title="w:Valencian language">Valencia</a></span></span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span> <span class="IPA nowrap">[ˈdi.a]</span></li>
<li><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r50165410" /><table class="audiotable" style="vertical-align: middle; display: inline-block; list-style: none; line-height: 1em; border-collapse: collapse; margin: 0;"><tbody><tr><td>Audio <span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="usage-label-accent"><span class="ib-content label-content">Catalonia</span></span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span><span class="ib-colon qualifier-colon">:</span></td><td class="audiofile"><span typeof="mw:File"><span><audio id="mwe_player_2" controls="" preload="none" data-mw-tmh="" class="mw-file-element" width="175" style="width:175px;" data-durationhint="1" data-mwtitle="LL-Q7026_(cat)-Unjoanqualsevol-dia.wav" data-mwprovider="wikimediacommons"><source src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/transcoded/8/80/LL-Q7026_%28cat%29-Unjoanqualsevol-dia.wav/LL-Q7026_%28cat%29-Unjoanqualsevol-dia.wav.ogg" type="audio/ogg; codecs=&quot;vorbis&quot;" data-transcodekey="ogg" data-width="0" data-height="0" /><source src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/transcoded/8/80/LL-Q7026_%28cat%29-Unjoanqualsevol-dia.wav/LL-Q7026_%28cat%29-Unjoanqualsevol-dia.wav.mp3" type="audio/mpeg" data-transcodekey="mp3" data-width="0" data-height="0" /><source src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/8/80/LL-Q7026_%28cat%29-Unjoanqualsevol-dia.wav" type="audio/wav" data-width="0" data-height="0" /></audio></span></span></td><td class="audiometa" style="font-size: 80%;">(<a href="/wiki/File:LL-Q7026_(cat)-Unjoanqualsevol-dia.wav" title="File:LL-Q7026 (cat)-Unjoanqualsevol-dia.wav">file</a>)</td></tr></tbody></table></li>
<li>Rhymes: <a href="/wiki/Rhymes:Catalan/ia" title="Rhymes:Catalan/ia"><span class="IPA nowrap">-ia</span></a></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=24" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="ca">dia</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="masculine gender">m</abbr></span> (<i>plural</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-ca p-form-of" lang="ca"><a href="/wiki/dies#Catalan" title="dies">dies</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li><a href="/wiki/day" title="day">day</a> <span class="mention-gloss-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss">period of 24 hours</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren">)</span>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>2011</b></span>, Tobies Grimaltos Mascarós, <cite>Idees i paraules: Una filosofia de la vida quotidiana</cite>, Universitat de València, <small><a href="/wiki/Special:BookSources/9788437084114" title="Special:BookSources/9788437084114">→ISBN</a></small>, page <span class="None" lang="und">41</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="ca">Avui és un <b>dia</b> normal. És un <b>dia</b> en el qual no res (m')ha passat especialment remarcable.</span><dl><dd><span class="e-translation">Today is a normal <b>day</b>. It's a <b>day</b> in which nothing especially remarkable happened (to me).</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/day" title="day">day</a> <span class="mention-gloss-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss">the part of the day between sunrise and sunset</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren">)</span>
<dl><dd><span class="nyms antonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Antonym:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="ca"><a href="/wiki/nit#Catalan" title="nit">nit</a></span></span></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>2011</b></span>, Cinto Niqui Espinosa, <cite>Fonaments i usos de tecnologia audiovisual digital</cite>, Editorial UOC, <small><a href="/wiki/Special:BookSources/9788497882903" title="Special:BookSources/9788497882903">→ISBN</a></small>, page <span class="None" lang="und">362</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="ca">En ona llarga durant el <b>dia</b>, a Catalunya, es poden escoltar les emissores Ràdio Montecarlo (RMC), als 216 kHz o Ràdio Alger, als 252 kHz.</span><dl><dd><span class="e-translation">In long wave during the <b>day</b>, in Catalonia, you can hear the broadcasters Ràdio Montecarlo (RMC) at 216 kHz or Ràdio Alger, at 252 kHz.</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Esperanto">Esperanto</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=35" title="Edit section: Esperanto"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_7">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=36" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <i class="Latn mention" lang="eo"><a href="/wiki/dio#Esperanto" title="dio">dio</a></i> +&#8206; <i class="Latn mention nowrap" lang="eo"><a href="/wiki/-a#Esperanto" title="-a">-a</a></i>.
</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Adjective">Adjective</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=38" title="Edit section: Adjective"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="eo">dia</strong> (<i>accusative singular</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-eo acc&#124;s-form-of" lang="eo"><a href="/wiki/dian#Esperanto" title="dian">dian</a></b>, <i>plural</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-eo p-form-of" lang="eo"><a href="/wiki/diaj#Esperanto" title="diaj">diaj</a></b>, <i>accusative plural</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-eo acc&#124;p-form-of" lang="eo"><a href="/wiki/diajn#Esperanto" title="diajn">diajn</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li><a href="/wiki/godly" title="godly">godly</a>, <a href="/wiki/divine" title="divine">divine</a> <span class="mention-gloss-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss">of or pertaining to <a href="/wiki/God#English" title="God">God</a> or <a href="/wiki/gods#English" title="gods">gods</a></span><span class="mention-gloss-paren">)</span></li></ol>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Finnish">Finnish</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=39" title="Edit section: Finnish"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_8">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=40" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Shortened from <i class="Latn mention" lang="fi"><a href="/wiki/diapositiivi#Finnish" title="diapositiivi">diapositiivi</a></i>, probably after the international example.
</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Interjection">Interjection</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=50" title="Edit section: Interjection"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="fr">dia</strong></span>
</p>
<ol><li><a href="/wiki/yah" title="yah">yah</a>!, cry to make (a) <a href="/wiki/working_animal" title="working animal">working animal</a>(s) etc. advance or turn left

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Guinea-Bissau_Creole">Guinea-Bissau Creole</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=53" title="Edit section: Guinea-Bissau Creole"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_9">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=54" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Portuguese_language" class="extiw" title="w:Portuguese language">Portuguese</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="pt"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Portuguese">dia</a></i>. Cognate with <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cape_Verdean_Creole" class="extiw" title="w:Cape Verdean Creole">Kabuverdianu</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="kea"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Kabuverdianu">dia</a></i>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_4">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=55" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="pov">dia</strong></span>
</p>
<ol><li><a href="/wiki/day" title="day">day</a> (period of 24 hours)</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/day" title="day">day</a> (period between sunrise and sunset)</li></ol>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Hungarian">Hungarian</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=56" title="Edit section: Hungarian"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_10">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=57" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#ellipsis" title="Appendix:Glossary">Ellipsis</a> of <i class="Latn mention" lang="hu"><a href="/wiki/diapozit%C3%ADv#Hungarian" title="diapozitív">diapozitív</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">diapositive</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, after the German <i class="Latn mention" lang="de"><a href="/wiki/Diapositiv#German" title="Diapositiv">Diapositiv</a></i>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation_8">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=58" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Hungarian_pronunciation" title="Appendix:Hungarian pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">[ˈdijɒ]</span></li>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_5">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=59" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Indonesian">Indonesian</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=66" title="Edit section: Indonesian"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation_10">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=67" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><span class="usage-label-accent"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indonesian_language#Phonology" class="extiw" title="w:Indonesian language">Standard Indonesian</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> <a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Indonesian_pronunciation" title="Appendix:Indonesian pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">/ˈdia/</span> <span class="IPA nowrap">[ˈdi.a]</span></li>
<li><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r50165410" /><table class="audiotable" style="vertical-align: middle; display: inline-block; list-style: none; line-height: 1em; border-collapse: collapse; margin: 0;"><tbody><tr><td>Audio<span class="ib-colon qualifier-colon">:</span></td><td class="audiofile"><span typeof="mw:File"><span><audio id="mwe_player_7" controls="" preload="none" data-mw-tmh="" class="mw-file-element" width="175" style="width:175px;" data-durationhint="2" data-mwtitle="LL-Q9240_(ind)-Xbypass-dia.wav" data-mwprovider="wikimediacommons"><source src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/transcoded/f/fc/LL-Q9240_%28ind%29-Xbypass-dia.wav/LL-Q9240_%28ind%29-Xbypass-dia.wav.ogg" type="audio/ogg; codecs=&quot;vorbis&quot;" data-transcodekey="ogg" data-width="0" data-height="0" /><source src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/transcoded/f/fc/LL-Q9240_%28ind%29-Xbypass-dia.wav/LL-Q9240_%28ind%29-Xbypass-dia.wav.mp3" type="audio/mpeg" data-transcodekey="mp3" data-width="0" data-height="0" /><source src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/f/fc/LL-Q9240_%28ind%29-Xbypass-dia.wav" type="audio/wav" data-width="0" data-height="0" /></audio></span></span></td><td class="audiometa" style="font-size: 80%;">(<a href="/wiki/File:LL-Q9240_(ind)-Xbypass-dia.wav" title="File:LL-Q9240 (ind)-Xbypass-dia.wav">file</a>)</td></tr></tbody></table></li>
<li>Rhymes: <a href="/wiki/Rhymes:Indonesian/a" title="Rhymes:Indonesian/a"><span class="IPA nowrap">-a</span></a></li>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_1">Etymology 1</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=68" title="Edit section: Etymology 1"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Malay_language" class="extiw" title="w:Malay language">Malay</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ms"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Malay">dia</a></i>, cognate with <i class="Latn mention" lang="id"><a href="/wiki/ia#Indonesian" title="ia">ia</a></i>, <i class="Latn mention nowrap" lang="id"><a href="/wiki/-nya#Indonesian" title="-nya">-nya</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Malayic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Malayic language">Proto-Malayic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="poz-mly-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Malayic/ia" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Malayic/ia">&#42;ia</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Malayo-Polynesian_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Malayo-Polynesian language">Proto-Malayo-Polynesian</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="poz-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Malayo-Polynesian/(si-)ia" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Malayo-Polynesian/(si-)ia">&#42;(si-)ia</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Austronesian_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Austronesian language">Proto-Austronesian</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="map-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Austronesian/(si-)ia" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Austronesian/(si-)ia">&#42;(si-)ia</a></i>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Pronoun_4">Pronoun</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=69" title="Edit section: Pronoun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Irish">Irish</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=75" title="Edit section: Irish"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation_11">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=76" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Irish_pronunciation" title="Appendix:Irish pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">/dʲiə/</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_1_2">Etymology 1</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=77" title="Edit section: Etymology 1"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Irish" class="extiw" title="w:Old Irish">Old Irish</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="sga"><a href="/wiki/d%C3%ADa#Old_Irish" title="día">día</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">god</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Celtic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Celtic language">Proto-Celtic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="cel-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Celtic/deiwos" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Celtic/deiwos">&#42;deiwos</a></i> (compare <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Welsh_language" class="extiw" title="w:Welsh language">Welsh</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="cy"><a href="/wiki/duw#Welsh" title="duw">duw</a></i>), from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Indo-European_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Indo-European language">Proto-Indo-European</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ine-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/deyw%C3%B3s" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/deywós">&#42;deywós</a></i> (compare <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sanskrit" class="extiw" title="w:Sanskrit">Sanskrit</a></span> <i class="Deva mention" lang="sa"><a href="/wiki/%E0%A4%A6%E0%A5%87%E0%A4%B5#Sanskrit" title="देव">देव</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span lang="sa-Latn" class="mention-tr tr Latn">deva</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Latin">Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/deus#Latin" title="deus">deus</a></i>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_English" class="extiw" title="w:Old English">Old English</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ang"><a href="/wiki/Tiw#Old_English" title="Tiw">Tīw</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">Germanic god of heroic glory</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>).
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Noun_6">Noun</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=78" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="ga">dia</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="masculine gender">m</abbr></span> (<i>genitive singular</i> <b class="Latn" lang="ga"><a href="/wiki/d%C3%A9#Irish" title="dé">dé</a></b>, <i>nominative plural</i> <b class="Latn" lang="ga"><a href="/wiki/d%C3%A9ithe#Irish" title="déithe">déithe</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li>a <a href="/wiki/god" title="god">god</a></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_2_3">Etymology 2</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=82" title="Edit section: Etymology 2"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Irish" class="extiw" title="w:Old Irish">Old Irish</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="sga"><a href="/wiki/d%C3%ADa#Old_Irish" title="día">día</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">day</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Celtic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Celtic language">Proto-Celtic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="cel-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Celtic/dy%C4%ABus" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Celtic/dyīus">&#42;dyīus</a></i> (compare <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Welsh_language" class="extiw" title="w:Welsh language">Welsh</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="cy"><a href="/wiki/dydd#Welsh" title="dydd">dydd</a></i>), from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Indo-European_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Indo-European language">Proto-Indo-European</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ine-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/dyew-" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/dyew-">&#42;dyew-</a></i>.
</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Ladino">Ladino</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=104" title="Edit section: Ladino"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_10">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=105" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="lad">dia</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="masculine gender">m</abbr></span> (<i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Hebrew_alphabet" title="Appendix:Hebrew alphabet">Hebrew spelling</a></i> <b class="Hebr" lang="lad"><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D7%93%D7%99%D7%90%D7%94&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="דיאה (page does not exist)">דיאה</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li><span class="form-of-definition use-with-mention">alternative spelling of <span class="form-of-definition-link"><i class="Latn mention" lang="lad"><a href="/wiki/diya#Ladino" title="diya">diya</a></i></span></span>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1910</b></span>, Reuben Eliyahu Israel, <cite>Traducsion libera de las poezias ebraicas de Roş Aşana i Kipur</cite>&#8206;<sup><a rel="nofollow" class="external autonumber" href="https://digitalcollections.lib.washington.edu/digital/collection/p16786coll3/id/4098">[2]</a></sup>, Craiova&#58; Institutul Grafic, I. Samitca şi D. Baraş, Socieatate in Comandita, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/1091680857">→OCLC</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://digitalcollections.lib.washington.edu/digital/collection/p16786coll3/id/4059">page <span class="None" lang="und">11</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="lad">En mi corason esta tu memoria santa<br /><b>Dia</b> i noce de mi no se aparta</span><dl><dd><span class="e-translation">In my heart lies your holy memory, <b>day</b> and night it separates not from me.</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_15">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=126" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation_14">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=127" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li class="mw-empty-elt"></li>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronoun_5">Pronoun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=128" title="Edit section: Pronoun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="ms">dia</strong> (<i>Jawi spelling</i> <b class="Arab ms-Arab" lang="ms"><a href="/wiki/%D8%AF%D9%8A#Malay" title="دي">دي</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li><span class="use-with-mention">Genderless third person pronoun&#59;</span> <a href="/wiki/he" title="he">he</a>, <a href="/wiki/she" title="she">she</a>, <a href="/wiki/it" title="it">it</a>.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="ms"><b>Dia</b> adik lelaki saya.</i><dl><dd><span class="e-translation"><b>He</b> is my younger brother.</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Mandarin">Mandarin</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=134" title="Edit section: Mandarin"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Romanization">Romanization</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=135" title="Edit section: Romanization"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="cmn">dia</strong></span>
</p>
<ol><li><span class="form-of-definition use-with-mention"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#nonstandard" title="Appendix:Glossary">nonstandard</a> spelling of <span class="form-of-definition-link"><i class="Latn mention" lang="cmn"><a href="/wiki/di%C7%8E#Mandarin" title="diǎ">diǎ</a></i></span></span></li></ol>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Usage_notes">Usage notes</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=136" title="Edit section: Usage notes"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/transcription" title="transcription">Transcriptions</a> of Mandarin into the Latin script often do not distinguish between the critical <a href="/wiki/tonal" title="tonal">tonal</a> differences employed in the Mandarin language, using words such as this one without indication of tone.</li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Middle_Irish">Middle Irish</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=137" title="Edit section: Middle Irish"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_16">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=138" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#univerbation" title="Appendix:Glossary">Univerbation</a> of <i class="Latn mention" lang="mga"><a href="/wiki/di#Middle_Irish" title="di">di</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">of/from</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span> +&#8206; <i class="Latn mention" lang="mga"><a href="/wiki/a/languages_M_to_Z#Middle_Irish" title="a/languages M to Z">a</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">his/her/its/their</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation_15">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=139" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Middle_Irish_phonology" class="extiw" title="wikipedia:Middle Irish phonology">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">/d̠ʲiə̯/</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Determiner">Determiner</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=140" title="Edit section: Determiner"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="mga">dia</strong> (<i>‘his’ and ‘its’ trigger lenition, ‘her’ triggers <span class="IPA nowrap">/h/</span>-prothesis, ‘their’ triggers eclipsis</i>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li>of/from his/her/its/their</li></ol>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Quotations">Quotations</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=141" title="Edit section: Quotations"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#c." title="Appendix:Glossary"><abbr title="circa">c.</abbr></a></i> <span class="None" lang="und"><b>1000</b></span>, “<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Tale_of_Mac_Da_Th%C3%B3%27s_Pig" class="extiw" title="w:The Tale of Mac Da Thó&#39;s Pig">The Tale of Mac Da Thó's Pig</a>”, in <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ernst_Windisch" class="extiw" title="w:Ernst Windisch">Ernst Windisch</a>, editor, <cite>Irische Texte</cite>, volume 1, published <span class="None" lang="und">1800</span>, section <a href="https://wikisource.org/wiki/Irische_Texte/1/Sc%C3%A9l_mucci_Mic_D%C3%A1th%C3%B3#s1" class="extiw" title="oldwikisource:Irische Texte/1/Scél mucci Mic Dáthó">1</a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="mga">Ailbe ainm in chon, ocus lan hEriu <b>dia</b> aurdarcus.</span><dl><dd><span class="e-translation">Ailbe was the dog’s name, and Ireland was full of his fame.</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_1_5">Etymology 1</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=155" title="Edit section: Etymology 1"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul class="etymid" id="Old&#95;Irish:&#95;of&#95;his" data-lang="sga" data-id="of his"></ul>
<p><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#univerbation" title="Appendix:Glossary">Univerbation</a> of <i class="Latn mention" lang="sga"><a href="/wiki/di#Old_Irish" title="di">di</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">of/from</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span> +&#8206; <i class="Latn mention" lang="sga"><a href="/wiki/a/languages_M_to_Z#Old_Irish" title="a/languages M to Z">a</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">his/her/its/their</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Pronunciation_17">Pronunciation</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=156" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Old_Irish_pronunciation" title="Appendix:Old Irish pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">[ˈdʲi.a]</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Determiner_3">Determiner</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=157" title="Edit section: Determiner"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="sga">dïa</strong> (<i>‘his’ and ‘its’ trigger lenition, ‘her’ triggers <span class="IPA nowrap">/h/</span>-prothesis, ‘their’ triggers eclipsis</i>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li>of/from his/her/its/their</li></ol>
<p><span class="see-cites">For quotations using this term, see <a href="/wiki/Citations:dia#Old_Irish" title="Citations:dia">Citations:dia</a>.</span>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_2_6">Etymology 2</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=158" title="Edit section: Etymology 2"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul class="etymid" id="Old&#95;Irish:&#95;of&#95;whom" data-lang="sga" data-id="of whom"></ul>
<p><i class="Latn mention" lang="sga"><a href="/wiki/di#Old_Irish" title="di">di</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">of/from</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span> +&#8206; <i class="Latn mention nowrap" lang="sga"><a href="/wiki/-a#Old_Irish:_relative" title="-a">-a</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="ann-pos">relative pronoun</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Pronunciation_18">Pronunciation</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=159" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Old_Irish_pronunciation" title="Appendix:Old Irish pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">[ˈdʲi.a]</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Pronoun_6">Pronoun</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=160" title="Edit section: Pronoun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="sga">dïa·</strong></span>
</p>
<ol><li>of/from whom/which</li></ol>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Conjunction">Conjunction</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=161" title="Edit section: Conjunction"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="sga">dïa</strong></span>
</p>
<ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content">with preterite</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> <a href="/wiki/when" title="when">when</a></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content">with subjunctive, positive only</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> <a href="/wiki/if" title="if">if</a>
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonym:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="sga"><a href="/wiki/m%C3%A1#Old_Irish" title="má">má</a></span></span></dd></dl></li></ol>
<p><span class="see-cites">For quotations using this term, see <a href="/wiki/Citations:dia#Old_Irish" title="Citations:dia">Citations:dia</a>.</span>
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Usage_notes_2">Usage notes</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=162" title="Edit section: Usage notes"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>The conjunction is followed by the appropriate dependent verbal form, applying the nasal mutation to it. Unlike modern Irish, no factual-counterfactual distinction exists in the use of <i class="Latn mention" lang="sga"><a href="/wiki/m%C3%A1#Old_Irish" title="má">má</a></i> and <i class="Latn mention" lang="sga"><strong class="selflink">dïa</strong></i>; they are completely interchangeable.
</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_19">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=176" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vulgar_Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Vulgar Latin">Vulgar Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Latin/dia" title="Reconstruction:Latin/dia">&#42;dia</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Latin">Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/dies#Latin" title="dies">diēs</a></i>.
</p>

</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_20">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=182" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#inherited" title="Appendix:Glossary">Inherited</a> from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vulgar_Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Vulgar Latin">Vulgar Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Latin/dia" title="Reconstruction:Latin/dia">&#42;dia</a></i>, first-declension reshaping of <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Classical_Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Classical Latin">Classical Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/dies#Latin" title="dies">diēs</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Italic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Italic language">Proto-Italic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="itc-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Italic/djous" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Italic/djous">&#42;djous</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Indo-European_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Indo-European language">Proto-Indo-European</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ine-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/dy%E1%B8%97ws" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/dyḗws">&#42;dyḗws</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">heaven, sky</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>. Compare <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Occitan" class="extiw" title="w:Old Occitan">Old Occitan</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="pro"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Old_Occitan">dia</a></i> and <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Galician%E2%80%93Portuguese" class="extiw" title="w:Galician–Portuguese">Old Galician-Portuguese</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="roa-opt"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Old_Galician-Portuguese">dia</a></i>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation_22">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=183" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Spanish_phonology" class="extiw" title="wikipedia:Old Spanish phonology">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">/ˈdia/</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_17">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=184" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="osp">dia</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="masculine gender">m</abbr></span></span>&#160;(<i>plural</i> <b><span class="Latn form-of lang-osp p-form-of" lang="osp"><a href="/wiki/dias#Old_Spanish" title="dias">dias</a></span></b>)
</p>
<ol><li><a href="/wiki/day" title="day">day</a>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#c." title="Appendix:Glossary"><abbr title="circa">c.</abbr></a></i> <span class="None" lang="und"><b>1200</b></span>, Almerich, <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fazienda_de_Ultramar" class="extiw" title="w:Fazienda de Ultramar">Fazienda de Ultramar</a></cite>, <span class="None" lang="und">f. 4v</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="osp">Qvãdo lo ſopo labã al <b>dia</b> t̃cero. q̃ ſe ẏua priſo de ſos amẏgos. E fue trã Jacob. Andadura .vij. <b>dias</b>. e alcãçol en el mõte de galaath.</span><dl><dd><span class="Latn e-normalization" lang="osp">&#91;Quando lo sopo Laban al día tercero, que se iva priso de sos amigos, e fue tras Jacob, andadura siete <b>días</b>, e alcançó-l en el monte de&#93;</span></dd><dd><span class="e-translation">When Laban came to know on the third <b>day</b> that he was fleeing, he took his relatives and went after Jacob, walking seven <b>days</b>, and he overtook him on the hill of Gilead.</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Papiamentu">Papiamentu</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=187" title="Edit section: Papiamentu"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_21">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=188" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Portuguese_language" class="extiw" title="w:Portuguese language">Portuguese</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="pt"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Portuguese">dia</a></i> and <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spanish_language" class="extiw" title="w:Spanish language">Spanish</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="es"><a href="/wiki/d%C3%ADa#Spanish" title="día">día</a></i> and <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cape_Verdean_Creole" class="extiw" title="w:Cape Verdean Creole">Kabuverdianu</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="kea"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Kabuverdianu">dia</a></i>.
</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_22">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=196" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#inherited" title="Appendix:Glossary">Inherited</a> from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Galician%E2%80%93Portuguese" class="extiw" title="w:Galician–Portuguese">Old Galician-Portuguese</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="roa-opt"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Old_Galician-Portuguese">dia</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vulgar_Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Vulgar Latin">Vulgar Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Latin/dia" title="Reconstruction:Latin/dia">&#42;dia</a></i>, first-declension reshaping of <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Classical_Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Classical Latin">Classical Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/dies#Latin" title="dies">diēs</a></i>, reformed from the accusative <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/diem#Latin" title="diem">diem</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Italic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Italic language">Proto-Italic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="itc-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Italic/dj%C4%93m" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Italic/djēm">&#42;djēm</a></i>, the accusative of <i class="Latn mention" lang="itc-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Italic/djous" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Italic/djous">&#42;djous</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">day, sky</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Indo-European_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Indo-European language">Proto-Indo-European</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ine-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/dy%E1%B8%97ws" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/dyḗws">&#42;dyḗws</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">heaven, sky</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>. Distantly related to <i class="Latn mention" lang="pt"><a href="/wiki/Deus#Portuguese" title="Deus">Deus</a></i>, <i class="Latn mention" lang="pt"><a href="/wiki/Zeus#Portuguese" title="Zeus">Zeus</a></i> and <i class="Latn mention" lang="pt"><a href="/wiki/Jove#Portuguese" title="Jove">Jove</a></i>.
</p>

<ul><li><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content qualifier-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_Portuguese" class="extiw" title="w:European Portuguese">Portugal</a></span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span> <a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Portuguese_pronunciation" title="Appendix:Portuguese pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">/ˈdi.ɐ/</span></li></ul></div>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_20">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=198" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="pt">dia</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="masculine gender">m</abbr></span> (<i>plural</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-pt p-form-of" lang="pt"><a href="/wiki/dias#Portuguese" title="dias">dias</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li><a href="/wiki/day" title="day">day</a>
<ol><li class="mw-empty-elt"></li><li class="senseid" id="Portuguese:&#95;period&#95;between&#95;sunrise&#95;and&#95;sunset" data-lang="pt" data-id="period between sunrise and sunset"> period between sunrise and sunset
<ul><li><b>1572</b>, Luís Vaz de Camões, <i>Os Lusíadas</i>, 5th canto:
<dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation" lang="pt"><span class="Latn" lang="pt">Trazia o Sol o<b> dia</b> celebrado &#47; Em que tres Reis das partes do Oriente,</span></span><dl><dd><span class="e-translation">The Sun brought the celebrated <b>day</b> / In which three Kings from the East,</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></li></ul></li>
<li class="mw-empty-elt"></li><li class="senseid" id="Portuguese:&#95;period&#95;from&#95;midnight&#95;to&#95;midnight" data-lang="pt" data-id="period from midnight to midnight"> period from midnight to the following midnight</li>
<li class="mw-empty-elt"></li><li class="senseid" id="Portuguese:&#95;24&#95;hours" data-lang="pt" data-id="24 hours"> period of 24 hours
<ul><li><b>1572</b>, Luís Vaz de Camões, <i>Os Lusíadas</i>, 5th canto:
<dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation" lang="pt"><span class="Latn" lang="pt">Mas logo ao outro <b>dia</b> ſeus parceiros &#47; Todos nús, &amp; da cor da eſcura treua,</span></span><dl><dd><span class="e-translation">But just the other <b>day</b> his partners / All naked, &amp; coloured as the dark darkness,</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></li></ul></li>
<li class="mw-empty-elt"></li><li class="senseid" id="Portuguese:&#95;rotational&#95;period&#95;of&#95;a&#95;planet" data-lang="pt" data-id="rotational period of a planet"> <span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/astronomy" title="astronomy">astronomy</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> rotational period of a planet</li>
<li class="mw-empty-elt"></li><li class="senseid" id="Portuguese:&#95;celebration&#95;date" data-lang="pt" data-id="celebration date"> <span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content">in phrases</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> date celebrating a particular thing, usually an event, profession or person</li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content">in phrases</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> an unspecified period of time either in the past or in the future</li></ol></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Scottish_Gaelic">Scottish Gaelic</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=205" title="Edit section: Scottish Gaelic"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_23">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=206" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Irish" class="extiw" title="w:Old Irish">Old Irish</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="sga"><a href="/wiki/d%C3%ADa#Old_Irish" title="día">día</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">god</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Celtic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Celtic language">Proto-Celtic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="cel-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Celtic/deiwos" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Celtic/deiwos">&#42;deiwos</a></i> (compare <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Welsh_language" class="extiw" title="w:Welsh language">Welsh</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="cy"><a href="/wiki/duw#Welsh" title="duw">duw</a></i>), from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Indo-European_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Indo-European language">Proto-Indo-European</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ine-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/deyw%C3%B3s" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/deywós">&#42;deywós</a></i> (compare <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sanskrit" class="extiw" title="w:Sanskrit">Sanskrit</a></span> <i class="Deva mention" lang="sa"><a href="/wiki/%E0%A4%A6%E0%A5%87%E0%A4%B5#Sanskrit" title="देव">देव</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span lang="sa-Latn" class="mention-tr tr Latn">deva</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Latin">Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/deus#Latin" title="deus">deus</a></i>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_English" class="extiw" title="w:Old English">Old English</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ang"><a href="/wiki/Tiw#Old_English" title="Tiw">Tīw</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">Germanic god of heroic glory</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>).
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation_24">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=207" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Scottish_Gaelic_pronunciation" title="Appendix:Scottish Gaelic pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">/tʲia/</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_21">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=208" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="gd">dia</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="masculine gender">m</abbr></span> (<i>genitive singular</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-gd gen&#124;s-form-of" lang="gd"><a href="/wiki/d%C3%A8#Scottish_Gaelic" title="dè">dè</a></b>, <i>plural</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-gd p-form-of" lang="gd"><a href="/wiki/diathan#Scottish_Gaelic" title="diathan">diathan</a></b> <i>or</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-gd p-form-of" lang="gd"><a href="/wiki/d%C3%A8e#Scottish_Gaelic" title="dèe">dèe</a></b>)</span>
</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Sicilian">Sicilian</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=213" title="Edit section: Sicilian"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Alternative_forms_9">Alternative forms</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=214" title="Edit section: Alternative forms"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><span class="Latn" lang="scn"><a href="/wiki/d%C3%ACa#Sicilian" title="dìa">dìa</a></span> <span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content">diacritic variant</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></li>
<li><span class="Latn" lang="scn"><a href="/wiki/ria#Sicilian" title="ria">ria</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="scn"><a href="/wiki/r%C3%ACa#Sicilian" title="rìa">rìa</a></span> <span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content">rhotacized, dialectal</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_24">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=215" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#inherited" title="Appendix:Glossary">Inherited</a> from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vulgar_Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Vulgar Latin">Vulgar Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Latin/dia" title="Reconstruction:Latin/dia">&#42;dia</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Latin">Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/dies#Latin" title="dies">diēs</a></i>, ultimately from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Indo-European_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Indo-European language">Proto-Indo-European</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ine-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/dy%E1%B8%97ws" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/dyḗws">&#42;dyḗws</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">heaven, sky</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>. Compare <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gascon_dialect" class="extiw" title="w:Gascon dialect">Gascon</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="oc"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Occitan">dia</a></i>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spanish_language" class="extiw" title="w:Spanish language">Spanish</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="es"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Spanish">dia</a></i> and <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_language" class="extiw" title="w:Italian language">Italian</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="it"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Italian">dia</a></i>~<i class="Latn mention" lang="it"><a href="/wiki/d%C3%AC#Italian" title="dì">dì</a></i>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation_25">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=216" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Sicilian_pronunciation" title="Appendix:Sicilian pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">/ˈdi.a/</span>, <span class="IPA nowrap">[ˈdi.a]</span>, <span class="IPA nowrap">[ˈɾi.a]</span></li>
<li>Hyphenation: <span class="Latn" lang="scn">dì‧a</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_22">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=217" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_25">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=224" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_24">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=226" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="srn">dia</strong></span>
</p>
<ol><li><span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/deer#English" title="deer">deer</a></span>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1936</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Melville_J._Herskovits" class="extiw" title="w:Melville J. Herskovits">Melville J. Herskovits</a>, Frances S. Herskovits, <cite>Suriname folk-lore</cite>&#8206;<sup><a rel="nofollow" class="external autonumber" href="https://www.dbnl.org/tekst/hers005suri01_01/hers005suri01_01_0042.php">[5]</a></sup>, New York&#58; Columbia University Press, page <span class="None" lang="und">424</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="srn">Bɔfru dɛ krei̯, <b>Dia</b> dɛ krei̯, Tamanwa 'ɛ krei̯. Nō mō ala den meti 'ɛ gowe wą' wą'. Nō mō Hagu drapɛ, 'ɛ bari, ‘Bia, bia, bia, &#47; Mi yɛre suma dɛdɛ, &#47; Ma karaki dɛ bro.’</span><dl><dd><span class="Latn e-normalization" lang="srn">&#91;Bofru e krei, <b>Dia</b> e krei, Tamanwa e krei. Nomo ala den meti e gwe wanwan. Nomo Agu drape e bari, 'Bia, bia, bia &#47; Mi yere suma dede &#47; Ma karaki e bro.'&#93;</span></dd><dd><span class="e-translation">Buffalo was crying, <b>Deer</b> was crying, Anteater was crying. No sooner did all the animals go away one by one, than Hog called out, ‘<i>Bia</i>, <i>bia</i>, <i>bia</i>, / I hear a person died, / But his backside breathes.’</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Swedish">Swedish</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=232" title="Edit section: Swedish"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_1_6">Etymology 1</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=233" title="Edit section: Etymology 1"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#inherited" title="Appendix:Glossary">Inherited</a> from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Swedish" class="extiw" title="w:Old Swedish">Old Swedish</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="gmq-osw"><a href="/wiki/di#Old_Swedish" title="di">di</a></i>, <i class="Latn mention" lang="gmq-osw"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Old_Swedish">dia</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Germanic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Germanic language">Proto-Germanic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="gem-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Germanic/dij%C5%8Dn%C4%85" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Germanic/dijōną">&#42;dijōną</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Indo-European_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Indo-European language">Proto-Indo-European</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ine-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/d%CA%B0eh%E2%82%81(y)-" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/dʰeh₁(y)-">&#42;dʰeh₁(y)-</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to suck, suckle</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>. 
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Verb_6">Verb</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=234" title="Edit section: Verb"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="sv">dia</strong> (<i>present</i> <b class="Latn" lang="sv"><a href="/wiki/diar#Swedish" title="diar">diar</a></b>, <i>preterite</i> <b class="Latn" lang="sv"><a href="/wiki/diade#Swedish" title="diade">diade</a></b>, <i>supine</i> <b class="Latn" lang="sv"><a href="/wiki/diat#Swedish" title="diat">diat</a></b>, <i>imperative</i> <b class="Latn" lang="sv"><strong class="selflink">dia</strong></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li>to <a href="/wiki/suckle" title="suckle">suckle</a></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Tswana">Tswana</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=247" title="Edit section: Tswana"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation_28">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=248" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tswana_phonology" class="extiw" title="wikipedia:Tswana phonology">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">/ˈdi.a/</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Verb_7">Verb</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=249" title="Edit section: Verb"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="tn">go dia</strong></span>
</p>
<ol><li>to <a href="/wiki/delay" title="delay">delay</a></li></ol>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Umbrian">Umbrian</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=250" title="Edit section: Umbrian"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_27">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=251" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Italic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Italic language">Proto-Italic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="itc-pro"><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reconstruction:Proto-Italic/dowj%C5%8D&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Italic/dowjō (page does not exist)">&#42;dowjō</a></i>. Compare also <i class="Ital mention" lang="xum"><a href="/wiki/%F0%90%8C%95%F0%90%8C%89%F0%90%8C%95%F0%90%8C%96#Umbrian" title="𐌕𐌉𐌕𐌖">𐌕𐌉𐌕𐌖</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span lang="xum-Latn" class="mention-tr tr Latn">titu</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span> and cognate <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin" class="extiw" title="w:Latin">Latin</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="la"><a href="/wiki/do#Latin" title="do">dō</a></i>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Verb_8">Verb</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=dia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=252" title="Edit section: Verb"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

</p>
